{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01Tikx2kHRV69DMm8kNzASQf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01Hjxo3zTYaunCVnQXJnNTT6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/analyze_reflow.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nReflow Process Analysis Script\nAnalyzes thermocouple data, MES logs, and defect data to answer compliance questions\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Any\n\n# Handbook specifications (extracted values)\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MIN = 100.0  # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_TAL = 30.0  # seconds\nMAX_TAL = 60.0  # seconds\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP = 203.0  # °C (liquidus + 20°C for quality reflow)\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Load thermocouple data\n    tc_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n\n    # Load MES log\n    mes_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n\n    # Load test defects\n    defects_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(tc_df)} thermocouple readings\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(mes_df)} MES log entries\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(defects_df)} defect records\")\n\n    return tc_df, mes_df, defects_df\n\n\ndef question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the thermocouple on the largest mass (tc_location = 'largest_mass').\n    Preheat zone is 100°C to 150°C.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No largest_mass thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Filter to preheat temperature range (100-150°C)\n        preheat_data = run_data[\n            (run_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n            (run_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            print(f\"Warning: Insufficient preheat data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        preheat_data['temp_diff'] = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n        preheat_data['time_diff'] = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n        preheat_data['ramp_rate'] = preheat_data['temp_diff'] / preheat_data['time_diff']\n\n        # Find maximum ramp rate (absolute value)\n        max_ramp = preheat_data['ramp_rate'].max()\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Store result\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - OK\")\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    Use solder_liquidus_c from MES log.\n    TAL range: 30-60 seconds.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No MES data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter to temperatures above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = run_data[run_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Calculate TAL\n        if len(above_liquidus) > 0:\n            tal = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n        else:\n            tal = 0.0\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: TAL = {tal:.2f}s - {status}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Minimum peak: liquidus + 20°C (approximately 203°C for Sn63Pb37).\n    Use the minimum peak across all thermocouples (coldest spot).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 3: Peak Temperature Compliance ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs from MES log (includes runs without TC data)\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + 20.0\n\n        # Get thermocouple data for this run\n        run_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"{run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAIL\")\n            continue\n\n        # Find peak temperature for each thermocouple\n        tc_peaks = run_data.groupby('tc_id')['temp_c'].max()\n\n        # Use minimum peak (coldest spot - most conservative)\n        min_peak = tc_peaks.min()\n        tc_at_min = tc_peaks.idxmin()\n\n        # Check compliance\n        is_failing = min_peak < required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_at_min,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if is_failing:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAIL\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASS\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n    Formula: MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n    Actual speed must be >= minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for idx, row in mes_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Required={required_min_speed:.2f}, Actual={actual_speed:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Ranking criteria (in priority order):\n    1. Must pass all compliance checks (Q1-Q4)\n    2. Zero critical defects\n    3. Highest yield\n    4. Zero total defects\n    5. Highest conveyor speed (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 5: Best Run Per Board Family ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q1_violations = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n    q2_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'compliant'}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q4_results if r['meets']}\n\n    # Get defect summary data\n    defect_summary = defects_df[defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    defect_summary_dict = {row['run_id']: row for idx, row in defect_summary.iterrows()}\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    families = sorted(mes_df['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for family in families:\n        family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family].copy()\n\n        # Score each run\n        scored_runs = []\n\n        for idx, run_row in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_row['run_id']\n\n            # Check compliance\n            passes_q1 = run_id not in q1_violations\n            passes_q2 = run_id in q2_compliant\n            passes_q3 = run_id not in q3_failing\n            passes_q4 = run_id in q4_compliant\n            all_compliant = passes_q1 and passes_q2 and passes_q3 and passes_q4\n\n            # Get defect data\n            defect_data = defect_summary_dict.get(run_id)\n            if defect_data is not None:\n                yield_pct = defect_data['fp_yield_pct']\n                units_scrap = defect_data['units_scrap']\n            else:\n                yield_pct = 0.0\n                units_scrap = 999  # Worst case\n\n            # Count critical defects\n            critical_defects = defects_df[\n                (defects_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                (defects_df['severity'] == 'critical')\n            ]['count'].sum()\n\n            # Calculate score (higher is better)\n            # Priority: compliance > zero critical > yield > speed\n            score = 0\n\n            if all_compliant:\n                score += 10000  # Huge bonus for compliance\n\n            if critical_defects == 0:\n                score += 1000  # Bonus for zero critical defects\n\n            score += yield_pct * 10  # Yield contribution\n\n            if units_scrap == 0:\n                score += 100  # Bonus for zero scrap\n\n            score += run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'] * 0.1  # Speed contribution\n\n            scored_runs.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': score,\n                'compliant': all_compliant,\n                'yield_pct': yield_pct,\n                'critical_defects': critical_defects,\n                'speed': run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        scored_runs.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['score'], x['run_id']))\n\n        # Best run is first, others are runners-up\n        best_run = scored_runs[0]['run_id']\n        runners_up = [r['run_id'] for r in scored_runs[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runners_up\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\n{family}:\")\n        print(f\"  Best: {best_run} (score: {scored_runs[0]['score']:.2f})\")\n        print(f\"  Runners-up: {runners_up}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Process Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Question 2: Time above liquidus\n    q2_results = question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 2 results saved to /app/output/q02.json\")\n\n    # Question 3: Peak temperature\n    q3_results = question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 3 results saved to /app/output/q03.json\")\n\n    # Question 4: Conveyor speed\n    q4_results = question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 4 results saved to /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n    # Load thermocouple data\n    tc_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n\n    # Load MES log\n    mes_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n\n    # Load test defects\n    defects_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(tc_df)} thermocouple readings\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(mes_df)} MES log entries\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(defects_df)} defect records\")\n\n    return tc_df, mes_df, defects_df\n\n\ndef question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the thermocouple on the largest mass (tc_location = 'largest_mass').\n    Preheat zone is 100°C to 150°C.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No largest_mass thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Filter to preheat temperature range (100-150°C)\n        preheat_data = run_data[\n            (run_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n            (run_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            print(f\"Warning: Insufficient preheat data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        preheat_data['temp_diff'] = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n        preheat_data['time_diff'] = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n        preheat_data['ramp_rate'] = preheat_data['temp_diff'] / preheat_data['time_diff']\n\n        # Find maximum ramp rate (absolute value)\n        max_ramp = preheat_data['ramp_rate'].max()\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Store result\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - OK\")\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    Use solder_liquidus_c from MES log.\n    TAL range: 30-60 seconds.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No MES data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter to temperatures above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = run_data[run_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Calculate TAL\n        if len(above_liquidus) > 0:\n            tal = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n        else:\n            tal = 0.0\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: TAL = {tal:.2f}s - {status}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Minimum peak: liquidus + 20°C (approximately 203°C for Sn63Pb37).\n    Use the minimum peak across all thermocouples (coldest spot).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 3: Peak Temperature Compliance ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs from MES log (includes runs without TC data)\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + 20.0\n\n        # Get thermocouple data for this run\n        run_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"{run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAIL\")\n            continue\n\n        # Find peak temperature for each thermocouple\n        tc_peaks = run_data.groupby('tc_id')['temp_c'].max()\n\n        # Use minimum peak (coldest spot - most conservative)\n        min_peak = tc_peaks.min()\n        tc_at_min = tc_peaks.idxmin()\n\n        # Check compliance\n        is_failing = min_peak < required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_at_min,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if is_failing:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAIL\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASS\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n    Formula: MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n    Actual speed must be >= minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for idx, row in mes_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Required={required_min_speed:.2f}, Actual={actual_speed:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Ranking criteria (in priority order):\n    1. Must pass all compliance checks (Q1-Q4)\n    2. Zero critical defects\n    3. Highest yield\n    4. Zero total defects\n    5. Highest conveyor speed (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 5: Best Run Per Board Family ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q1_violations = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n    q2_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'compliant'}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q4_results if r['meets']}\n\n    # Get defect summary data\n    defect_summary = defects_df[defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    defect_summary_dict = {row['run_id']: row for idx, row in defect_summary.iterrows()}\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    families = sorted(mes_df['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for family in families:\n        family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family].copy()\n\n        # Score each run\n        scored_runs = []\n\n        for idx, run_row in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_row['run_id']\n\n            # Check compliance\n            passes_q1 = run_id not in q1_violations\n            passes_q2 = run_id in q2_compliant\n            passes_q3 = run_id not in q3_failing\n            passes_q4 = run_id in q4_compliant\n            all_compliant = passes_q1 and passes_q2 and passes_q3 and passes_q4\n\n            # Get defect data\n            defect_data = defect_summary_dict.get(run_id)\n            if defect_data is not None:\n                yield_pct = defect_data['fp_yield_pct']\n                units_scrap = defect_data['units_scrap']\n            else:\n                yield_pct = 0.0\n                units_scrap = 999  # Worst case\n\n            # Count critical defects\n            critical_defects = defects_df[\n                (defects_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                (defects_df['severity'] == 'critical')\n            ]['count'].sum()\n\n            # Calculate score (higher is better)\n            # Priority: compliance > zero critical > yield > speed\n            score = 0\n\n            if all_compliant:\n                score += 10000  # Huge bonus for compliance\n\n            if critical_defects == 0:\n                score += 1000  # Bonus for zero critical defects\n\n            score += yield_pct * 10  # Yield contribution\n\n            if units_scrap == 0:\n                score += 100  # Bonus for zero scrap\n\n            score += run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'] * 0.1  # Speed contribution\n\n            scored_runs.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': score,\n                'compliant': all_compliant,\n                'yield_pct': yield_pct,\n                'critical_defects': critical_defects,\n                'speed': run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        scored_runs.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['score'], x['run_id']))\n\n        # Best run is first, others are runners-up\n        best_run = scored_runs[0]['run_id']\n        runners_up = [r['run_id'] for r in scored_runs[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runners_up\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\n{family}:\")\n        print(f\"  Best: {best_run} (score: {scored_runs[0]['score']:.2f})\")\n        print(f\"  Runners-up: {runners_up}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Process Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load data\n    tc_df, mes_df, defects_df = load_data()\n\n    # Question 1: Maximum preheat ramp rate\n    q1_results = question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 1 results saved to /app/output/q01.json\")\n\n    # Question 2: Time above liquidus\n    q2_results = question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 2 results saved to /app/output/q02.json\")\n\n    # Question 3: Peak temperature\n    q3_results = question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 3 results saved to /app/output/q03.json\")\n\n    # Question 4: Conveyor speed\n    q4_results = question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 4 results saved to /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n    # Question 5: Best run per family\n    q5_results = question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results)\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 5 results saved to /app/output/q05.json\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Analysis complete!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Filter to temperatures above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = run_data[run_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Calculate TAL\n        if len(above_liquidus) > 0:\n            tal = float(above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min())\n        else:\n            tal = 0.0\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: TAL = {tal:.2f}s - {status}\")\n\n    return results","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nReflow Process Analysis Script\nAnalyzes thermocouple data, MES logs, and defect data to answer compliance questions\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Any\n\n# Handbook specifications (extracted values)\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MIN = 100.0  # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_TAL = 30.0  # seconds\nMAX_TAL = 60.0  # seconds\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP = 203.0  # °C (liquidus + 20°C for quality reflow)\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Load thermocouple data\n    tc_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n\n    # Load MES log\n    mes_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n\n    # Load test defects\n    defects_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(tc_df)} thermocouple readings\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(mes_df)} MES log entries\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(defects_df)} defect records\")\n\n    return tc_df, mes_df, defects_df\n\n\ndef question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the thermocouple on the largest mass (tc_location = 'largest_mass').\n    Preheat zone is 100°C to 150°C.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No largest_mass thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Filter to preheat temperature range (100-150°C)\n        preheat_data = run_data[\n            (run_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n            (run_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            print(f\"Warning: Insufficient preheat data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        preheat_data['temp_diff'] = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n        preheat_data['time_diff'] = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n        preheat_data['ramp_rate'] = preheat_data['temp_diff'] / preheat_data['time_diff']\n\n        # Find maximum ramp rate (absolute value)\n        max_ramp = preheat_data['ramp_rate'].max()\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Store result\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - OK\")\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    Use solder_liquidus_c from MES log.\n    TAL range: 30-60 seconds.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No MES data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter to temperatures above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = run_data[run_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Calculate TAL\n        if len(above_liquidus) > 0:\n            tal = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n        else:\n            tal = 0.0\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: TAL = {tal:.2f}s - {status}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Minimum peak: liquidus + 20°C (approximately 203°C for Sn63Pb37).\n    Use the minimum peak across all thermocouples (coldest spot).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 3: Peak Temperature Compliance ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs from MES log (includes runs without TC data)\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + 20.0\n\n        # Get thermocouple data for this run\n        run_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"{run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAIL\")\n            continue\n\n        # Find peak temperature for each thermocouple\n        tc_peaks = run_data.groupby('tc_id')['temp_c'].max()\n\n        # Use minimum peak (coldest spot - most conservative)\n        min_peak = tc_peaks.min()\n        tc_at_min = tc_peaks.idxmin()\n\n        # Check compliance\n        is_failing = min_peak < required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_at_min,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if is_failing:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAIL\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASS\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n    Formula: MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n    Actual speed must be >= minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for idx, row in mes_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Required={required_min_speed:.2f}, Actual={actual_speed:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Ranking criteria (in priority order):\n    1. Must pass all compliance checks (Q1-Q4)\n    2. Zero critical defects\n    3. Highest yield\n    4. Zero total defects\n    5. Highest conveyor speed (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 5: Best Run Per Board Family ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q1_violations = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n    q2_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'compliant'}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q4_results if r['meets']}\n\n    # Get defect summary data\n    defect_summary = defects_df[defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    defect_summary_dict = {row['run_id']: row for idx, row in defect_summary.iterrows()}\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    families = sorted(mes_df['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for family in families:\n        family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family].copy()\n\n        # Score each run\n        scored_runs = []\n\n        for idx, run_row in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_row['run_id']\n\n            # Check compliance\n            passes_q1 = run_id not in q1_violations\n            passes_q2 = run_id in q2_compliant\n            passes_q3 = run_id not in q3_failing\n            passes_q4 = run_id in q4_compliant\n            all_compliant = passes_q1 and passes_q2 and passes_q3 and passes_q4\n\n            # Get defect data\n            defect_data = defect_summary_dict.get(run_id)\n            if defect_data is not None:\n                yield_pct = defect_data['fp_yield_pct']\n                units_scrap = defect_data['units_scrap']\n            else:\n                yield_pct = 0.0\n                units_scrap = 999  # Worst case\n\n            # Count critical defects\n            critical_defects = defects_df[\n                (defects_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                (defects_df['severity'] == 'critical')\n            ]['count'].sum()\n\n            # Calculate score (higher is better)\n            # Priority: compliance > zero critical > yield > speed\n            score = 0\n\n            if all_compliant:\n                score += 10000  # Huge bonus for compliance\n\n            if critical_defects == 0:\n                score += 1000  # Bonus for zero critical defects\n\n            score += yield_pct * 10  # Yield contribution\n\n            if units_scrap == 0:\n                score += 100  # Bonus for zero scrap\n\n            score += run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'] * 0.1  # Speed contribution\n\n            scored_runs.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': score,\n                'compliant': all_compliant,\n                'yield_pct': yield_pct,\n                'critical_defects': critical_defects,\n                'speed': run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        scored_runs.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['score'], x['run_id']))\n\n        # Best run is first, others are runners-up\n        best_run = scored_runs[0]['run_id']\n        runners_up = [r['run_id'] for r in scored_runs[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runners_up\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\n{family}:\")\n        print(f\"  Best: {best_run} (score: {scored_runs[0]['score']:.2f})\")\n        print(f\"  Runners-up: {runners_up}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Process Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load data\n    tc_df, mes_df, defects_df = load_data()\n\n    # Question 1: Maximum preheat ramp rate\n    q1_results = question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 1 results saved to /app/output/q01.json\")\n\n    # Question 2: Time above liquidus\n    q2_results = question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 2 results saved to /app/output/q02.json\")\n\n    # Question 3: Peak temperature\n    q3_results = question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 3 results saved to /app/output/q03.json\")\n\n    # Question 4: Conveyor speed\n    q4_results = question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 4 results saved to /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n    # Question 5: Best run per family\n    q5_results = question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results)\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 5 results saved to /app/output/q05.json\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Analysis complete!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":124,"oldLines":7,"newStart":124,"newLines":7,"lines":["             print(f\"Warning: No MES data for {run_id}\")","             continue"," ","-        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]","+        liquidus_temp = float(mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])"," ","         # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_TAL = 30.0  # seconds\nMAX_TAL = 60.0  # seconds\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP = 203.0  # °C (liquidus + 20°C for quality reflow)\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Load thermocouple data\n    tc_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n\n    # Load MES log\n    mes_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n\n    # Load test defects\n    defects_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(tc_df)} thermocouple readings\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(mes_df)} MES log entries\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(defects_df)} defect records\")\n\n    return tc_df, mes_df, defects_df\n\n\ndef question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the thermocouple on the largest mass (tc_location = 'largest_mass').\n    Preheat zone is 100°C to 150°C.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No largest_mass thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Filter to preheat temperature range (100-150°C)\n        preheat_data = run_data[\n            (run_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n            (run_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            print(f\"Warning: Insufficient preheat data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        preheat_data['temp_diff'] = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n        preheat_data['time_diff'] = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n        preheat_data['ramp_rate'] = preheat_data['temp_diff'] / preheat_data['time_diff']\n\n        # Find maximum ramp rate (absolute value)\n        max_ramp = preheat_data['ramp_rate'].max()\n        tc_id = run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Store result\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - OK\")\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    Use solder_liquidus_c from MES log.\n    TAL range: 30-60 seconds.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No MES data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = float(mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = str(run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Filter to temperatures above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = run_data[run_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Calculate TAL\n        if len(above_liquidus) > 0:\n            tal = float(above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min())\n        else:\n            tal = 0.0\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: TAL = {tal:.2f}s - {status}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Minimum peak: liquidus + 20°C (approximately 203°C for Sn63Pb37).\n    Use the minimum peak across all thermocouples (coldest spot).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 3: Peak Temperature Compliance ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs from MES log (includes runs without TC data)\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + 20.0\n\n        # Get thermocouple data for this run\n        run_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"{run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAIL\")\n            continue\n\n        # Find peak temperature for each thermocouple\n        tc_peaks = run_data.groupby('tc_id')['temp_c'].max()\n\n        # Use minimum peak (coldest spot - most conservative)\n        min_peak = tc_peaks.min()\n        tc_at_min = tc_peaks.idxmin()\n\n        # Check compliance\n        is_failing = min_peak < required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_at_min,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if is_failing:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAIL\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASS\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n    Formula: MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n    Actual speed must be >= minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for idx, row in mes_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Required={required_min_speed:.2f}, Actual={actual_speed:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Ranking criteria (in priority order):\n    1. Must pass all compliance checks (Q1-Q4)\n    2. Zero critical defects\n    3. Highest yield\n    4. Zero total defects\n    5. Highest conveyor speed (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 5: Best Run Per Board Family ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q1_violations = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n    q2_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'compliant'}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q4_results if r['meets']}\n\n    # Get defect summary data\n    defect_summary = defects_df[defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    defect_summary_dict = {row['run_id']: row for idx, row in defect_summary.iterrows()}\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    families = sorted(mes_df['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for family in families:\n        family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family].copy()\n\n        # Score each run\n        scored_runs = []\n\n        for idx, run_row in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_row['run_id']\n\n            # Check compliance\n            passes_q1 = run_id not in q1_violations\n            passes_q2 = run_id in q2_compliant\n            passes_q3 = run_id not in q3_failing\n            passes_q4 = run_id in q4_compliant\n            all_compliant = passes_q1 and passes_q2 and passes_q3 and passes_q4\n\n            # Get defect data\n            defect_data = defect_summary_dict.get(run_id)\n            if defect_data is not None:\n                yield_pct = defect_data['fp_yield_pct']\n                units_scrap = defect_data['units_scrap']\n            else:\n                yield_pct = 0.0\n                units_scrap = 999  # Worst case\n\n            # Count critical defects\n            critical_defects = defects_df[\n                (defects_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                (defects_df['severity'] == 'critical')\n            ]['count'].sum()\n\n            # Calculate score (higher is better)\n            # Priority: compliance > zero critical > yield > speed\n            score = 0\n\n            if all_compliant:\n                score += 10000  # Huge bonus for compliance\n\n            if critical_defects == 0:\n                score += 1000  # Bonus for zero critical defects\n\n            score += yield_pct * 10  # Yield contribution\n\n            if units_scrap == 0:\n                score += 100  # Bonus for zero scrap\n\n            score += run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'] * 0.1  # Speed contribution\n\n            scored_runs.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': score,\n                'compliant': all_compliant,\n                'yield_pct': yield_pct,\n                'critical_defects': critical_defects,\n                'speed': run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        scored_runs.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['score'], x['run_id']))\n\n        # Best run is first, others are runners-up\n        best_run = scored_runs[0]['run_id']\n        runners_up = [r['run_id'] for r in scored_runs[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runners_up\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\n{family}:\")\n        print(f\"  Best: {best_run} (score: {scored_runs[0]['score']:.2f})\")\n        print(f\"  Runners-up: {runners_up}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Process Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Question 2: Time above liquidus\n    q2_results = question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 2 results saved to /app/output/q02.json\")\n\n    # Question 3: Peak temperature\n    q3_results = question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 3 results saved to /app/output/q03.json\")\n\n    # Question 4: Conveyor speed\n    q4_results = question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 4 results saved to /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n    # Question 5: Best run per family\n    q5_results = question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results)\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 5 results saved to /app/output/q05.json\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Analysis complete!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":81,"oldLines":8,"newStart":81,"newLines":8,"lines":["         preheat_data['ramp_rate'] = preheat_data['temp_diff'] / preheat_data['time_diff']"," ","         # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_TAL = 30.0  # seconds\nMAX_TAL = 60.0  # seconds\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP = 203.0  # °C (liquidus + 20°C for quality reflow)\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Load thermocouple data\n    tc_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n\n    # Load MES log\n    mes_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n\n    # Load test defects\n    defects_df = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(tc_df)} thermocouple readings\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(mes_df)} MES log entries\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(defects_df)} defect records\")\n\n    return tc_df, mes_df, defects_df\n\n\ndef question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the thermocouple on the largest mass (tc_location = 'largest_mass').\n    Preheat zone is 100°C to 150°C.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No largest_mass thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Filter to preheat temperature range (100-150°C)\n        preheat_data = run_data[\n            (run_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n            (run_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            print(f\"Warning: Insufficient preheat data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        preheat_data['temp_diff'] = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n        preheat_data['time_diff'] = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n        preheat_data['ramp_rate'] = preheat_data['temp_diff'] / preheat_data['time_diff']\n\n        # Find maximum ramp rate (absolute value)\n        max_ramp = float(preheat_data['ramp_rate'].max())\n        tc_id = str(run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Store result\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: {max_ramp:.2f} °C/s - OK\")\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    Use solder_liquidus_c from MES log.\n    TAL range: 30-60 seconds.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    runs = sorted(tc_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No MES data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = float(mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Filter to this run and largest_mass thermocouple\n        run_data = tc_df[\n            (tc_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n            (tc_df['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass')\n        ].copy()\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            print(f\"Warning: No thermocouple data for {run_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = str(run_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Filter to temperatures above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = run_data[run_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Calculate TAL\n        if len(above_liquidus) > 0:\n            tal = float(above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min())\n        else:\n            tal = 0.0\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: TAL = {tal:.2f}s - {status}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Minimum peak: liquidus + 20°C (approximately 203°C for Sn63Pb37).\n    Use the minimum peak across all thermocouples (coldest spot).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 3: Peak Temperature Compliance ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs from MES log (includes runs without TC data)\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_df[mes_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + 20.0\n\n        # Get thermocouple data for this run\n        run_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"{run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAIL\")\n            continue\n\n        # Find peak temperature for each thermocouple\n        tc_peaks = run_data.groupby('tc_id')['temp_c'].max()\n\n        # Use minimum peak (coldest spot - most conservative)\n        min_peak = tc_peaks.min()\n        tc_at_min = tc_peaks.idxmin()\n\n        # Check compliance\n        is_failing = min_peak < required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_at_min,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if is_failing:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAIL\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASS\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n    Formula: MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n    Actual speed must be >= minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for idx, row in mes_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # MIN_SPEED = (boards_per_min × board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Required={required_min_speed:.2f}, Actual={actual_speed:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Ranking criteria (in priority order):\n    1. Must pass all compliance checks (Q1-Q4)\n    2. Zero critical defects\n    3. Highest yield\n    4. Zero total defects\n    5. Highest conveyor speed (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Question 5: Best Run Per Board Family ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q1_violations = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n    q2_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'compliant'}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_compliant = {r['run_id'] for r in q4_results if r['meets']}\n\n    # Get defect summary data\n    defect_summary = defects_df[defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    defect_summary_dict = {row['run_id']: row for idx, row in defect_summary.iterrows()}\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    families = sorted(mes_df['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for family in families:\n        family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family].copy()\n\n        # Score each run\n        scored_runs = []\n\n        for idx, run_row in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_row['run_id']\n\n            # Check compliance\n            passes_q1 = run_id not in q1_violations\n            passes_q2 = run_id in q2_compliant\n            passes_q3 = run_id not in q3_failing\n            passes_q4 = run_id in q4_compliant\n            all_compliant = passes_q1 and passes_q2 and passes_q3 and passes_q4\n\n            # Get defect data\n            defect_data = defect_summary_dict.get(run_id)\n            if defect_data is not None:\n                yield_pct = defect_data['fp_yield_pct']\n                units_scrap = defect_data['units_scrap']\n            else:\n                yield_pct = 0.0\n                units_scrap = 999  # Worst case\n\n            # Count critical defects\n            critical_defects = defects_df[\n                (defects_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                (defects_df['severity'] == 'critical')\n            ]['count'].sum()\n\n            # Calculate score (higher is better)\n            # Priority: compliance > zero critical > yield > speed\n            score = 0\n\n            if all_compliant:\n                score += 10000  # Huge bonus for compliance\n\n            if critical_defects == 0:\n                score += 1000  # Bonus for zero critical defects\n\n            score += yield_pct * 10  # Yield contribution\n\n            if units_scrap == 0:\n                score += 100  # Bonus for zero scrap\n\n            score += run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'] * 0.1  # Speed contribution\n\n            scored_runs.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': score,\n                'compliant': all_compliant,\n                'yield_pct': yield_pct,\n                'critical_defects': critical_defects,\n                'speed': run_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        scored_runs.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['score'], x['run_id']))\n\n        # Best run is first, others are runners-up\n        best_run = scored_runs[0]['run_id']\n        runners_up = [r['run_id'] for r in scored_runs[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runners_up\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\n{family}:\")\n        print(f\"  Best: {best_run} (score: {scored_runs[0]['score']:.2f})\")\n        print(f\"  Runners-up: {runners_up}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Reflow Process Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load data\n    tc_df, mes_df, defects_df = load_data()\n\n    # Question 1: Maximum preheat ramp rate\n    q1_results = question_1_max_preheat_ramp(tc_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 1 results saved to /app/output/q01.json\")\n\n    # Question 2: Time above liquidus\n    q2_results = question_2_time_above_liquidus(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 2 results saved to /app/output/q02.json\")\n\n    # Question 3: Peak temperature\n    q3_results = question_3_peak_temperature(tc_df, mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 3 results saved to /app/output/q03.json\")\n\n    # Question 4: Conveyor speed\n    q4_results = question_4_conveyor_speed(mes_df)\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 4 results saved to /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n    # Question 5: Best run per family\n    q5_results = question_5_best_run_per_family(mes_df, defects_df, q1_results, q2_results, q3_results, q4_results)\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nQuestion 5 results saved to /app/output/q05.json\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Analysis complete!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":184,"oldLines":7,"newStart":184,"newLines":7,"lines":["     for run_id in all_runs:","         #
